MINISTRY OF LAW, JUSTICE AND COMPANY 
AFFAIRS 


(Department of Company Affairs) 
New Delhi, the 16th April, 1984 


■ COST ACCOUNTING RECORDS (BULK DRUGS) 
AMENDMENT RULES, 1984 
G.S.R 505.—In exercise of powers conferred by sub¬ 
section fl) of section 642, read with clause (d) of 
tion (l) ‘of section 209 of the Comparucs Act, 1956 (1 of 
1956), the Central Government hereby makes the fooo^'iip 
rules furthw to amend the Cost AccoimtinE Records (Bulk 
Dmosl Rules. 1974 namely ;— 


1, (1) These rules may be called the Cost Accounting Re¬ 
cords (Bulk Drugs) (Amendment) Rules. 1984. 





1169 


tnraTrtntw: nf 26 , i, leoe 


f^fprll—3(1)] 


(2) They shall come into force on the date pf their 
publication in the Official Gaiette. 

2. In tJic Cost Accounting Records (Bulk Drug) Rules, 
1974 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules), for rule 2. 
the following shall be substituted, namely :— 

“2. Application.—They shall apply to every company 
engaged in the production, processing or manufac¬ 
ture of bulk drugs and/or intermediat required 
specifically for production of bulk drugs or both”. 

3. In rule 4 of the said rules :— 

(i) in sub-rule (1), for the words, “Bulk drug” and “bulk 

drugs” wherever they occur, the words “bulk drug 
and/or intermediate” and “bulk drugs and/or in¬ 
termediate” shall respectively be substituted; 

(ii) for sub-rule (2), the following shall be substituted, 

namely :— 

“(2) The books of accounts referred to in sub-rule (1) 
shall be kept on a regular basis so ns to make it 
possible to calculate the cost of production and 
cost of soles of each of the bulk drugs and/or 
intermediates at regular lnterval8> say quarterly 
during the financial year (hereinafter referred to 
as the relevant period) as well as for the financial 
year as a whole, from the particulars entered 
therein and every such books of accounts and the 
performance specified in Schedule n shall be 
completed within ninety days from the ^d of the 
financial year of the company to which they 
relate”. 

4. In Schedule I of the said rules ;— 

(1) In para I, in sub-para (a), <b), (c) for the words 
“bulk drug” and “bulk drugs” wherever they occur, 
the words “bulk drug and/or intermediate” and 
“bulk drugs and/or intermediates” shall be respecti¬ 
vely be sulMtltuted; 

(ii) in para II, in sub-para (a), for the words ‘bulk drug” 

the words “bulk drug and/or intermediate” shall be 
substituted; 

(iii) in para VUI, in sub-para (c), for the words “bulk 
drugs”, the words “bulk drugs and/or intermedfa- 
tes” shall be substithted. 

(iv) in Para X, for the words “bulk drugs or interme¬ 
diates”, the words “bulk drugs and/or interme¬ 
diates” shall be substituted. 

(v) in para XI, for the words “bulk drugs”, the words 

“bulk drugs and/or intermediates” shall be substi¬ 
tuted. 

(vi) in para XUI ;— 

(a) in sub-para (a), for the words “bulk drugs and 

intermediates” and “bulk drugs or intermediates” 
the words “bulk drugs and/or intermediates” shall 
be substituted; 

(b) in sub-para (b), for the words “bulk drugs” or 

“bulk drugs and intermediates” wherever they 
occur, the words “bulk drugs and/or interme¬ 
diates” shall be substituted; 

. (vii) in para XV, for the words “bulk drug and inter¬ 
mediates”, the words “bulk drugs and/or interme¬ 
diates” shall he substituted: 

(viii) in para XVII :—i 

(a) for the opening para, the following shall be substi¬ 
tuted, namely:— 

“Cost statements showing separately the actual 
cost of production and marketing of bulk drugs 


and/or Intermediates which are cither partial 
or fuUy utilised for the manufacture of bu, 
drugs or sold by the company, shal) be shou 
in proforma ‘B’ and ‘C’ of Schedule U or 
any form as near thereto as practicable. (2oft 
of bulk dru^s and/or intermediates if exporter 
shall be exhibited in separate cost statement 
the same excluded from the cost statements 
those products sold in the internal market”; 

(b) in the last para, for the words “intermediate 

bulk drug” and “intermediate or bulk drug”, th 
words “bulk drug and/or intermediate’’ shall b 
substituted. 

(ix) in para XK^ for the words “bulk drugs” the word) 
"bulk drugs, and/or inVermediates” shall be lUb- 
stituted; 

(x) in para XXI, for the words "bulk drugs” whcrevei 
they occur, th* words “bulk drugs and/or inter 
mediates” ^nll be substituted, 

5. In Schedule II of the said rules.—(i) in proforma ‘A’, 
for the words *namo of the bulk drug fo be inserted"., 
the words "’'‘*’*'nan» of the bulk drug and/or jntermediajo" 
be sub.nituted; 

(ii) in profoima ‘C ■ , 

(a) in the heading, for the words "*'*'namc of the bulk 
drug to be shown”, words "’•'''♦name of the bulk 
drug/infermedlate to be shown” shall be substituted. 

(b) in the table (1) under column I, against item J, 
for the words "cost of naked bulk drug as pef pro¬ 
forma ‘B’ ”, the words "cost of naked bulk drug/ 
intermediate as per proforma ’B’ ” shall be substi¬ 
tuted, 

(2) under column 1, against ii’em 3, for the words 
"total cost of packed bulk drug”, the words "total 
cost of packed bulk drug/intermediate” shall be 
substituted: 

(3) in foot note No. 1, for the words "bulk drug” the 
words'“bulk drug/iniermediatc” shall bo substituted. 

(4) in foot note No. 4, for the words "bulk drugs”, the 
words "bulk drugs and/or intermediates” shall be 
subsfftufed. 

(5) foot note No. 9, shall be omitted. 

(iii) In proforma ‘D’ Under particulars in i(tra No. (2) 
and (3) for words "bulk drugs” the words "bulk drugs 
and/or intermediates" shall be substituted. 

(iv) In proforma ‘E’ —(a) under the heading for the 

words "Statement showing the total expenses incuired dur¬ 
ing the year..and share applicable to bulk drugs”, 

the Words "Statement showing the total expenses incuired 

during the year...and share applicable 

to bulk drugs and/or intermediates” shall be subatltut^; 

(b) below proforma ‘E’, for the words "♦*to be indicat¬ 
ed separately for quantities of bulk drugs”, the words “**to 
be indicated separately for quantities of bulk drugs/lnter- 
medlates" shall be sut»tituted. 

Note : Principal rules were puNished vide Notification 
No. GSR 130(E) dated 14-3-1974, published in the Gazette 
of India, Extraordinary, Part H, Section 3 sub-section (1), 
pages 497 to 511.

New Delhi, the 10th May, 1984 
COST ACCOUNTING RECORDS (STEEL TUBES AND 
PIPES) RULES, 1984 

G.S.R. 506.—in exercise of the powers gonefired ijV sub¬ 
section (1) of section 642 read with, clause (d) of sub¬ 
section (1) of section 2(>9 of the Companies Act, 1956 (1 
of 1956) hereinafter referred to as. the said Act, the Central 
Government hereby makes the following rules, namely ;— 

1 Short title and commencement.—(1) These rules may he 
called the Cost Accounting Records (Steel Dibe, and Pipes) 
Rules, 1984. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette'. 

2. Application.—The.se rules shall apply to every com¬ 
pany engaged in the production, processing or manufacture 
of Steel Tubes and Pipes (including Stainless Steel) both 
black and galvanised and in various sizes, shapes and quali¬ 
ties excepting those companies falling under the category of 
‘vSmall Scale Industrial Units’. 

Explanation : 

For the puiTxtse of thin rule, the expression ‘Small Scale 
Industrial .Unit’ means any industrial undertaking registered 
with the Directorate of Industries or Small Scale Industries, 
as the case may be, of tire State Governmenf in respect of 


[It'S . 52 / 74 / 77 -tof Fo too] 
n^o pgo Rfto 

which the investment in plant and machinery Ig not in ex¬ 
cess of twenty lacs of rupees in value. 

3. Maintenance of Records,—(1) Every company to which 
these rules apply shall, in respect of each of its.financial year 
commencing on or after the commencement of these rules, 
keep proper hooks of accounts containing, inter-alia, the 
particulars specified in Schedule I and Schedule II annexed 
to these Rules or in a l''orni a* near thereto as practicable, 
relating to utilisation of material, labour and other items of 
cost in so far as these are applicable to Steel Tubes and 
Pipes ircferred vb in Rule 2. 

Provided that if the said company is manufacturing any 
other product(ii) or is engaged in other activities in addition 
to item referred to in Rule 2, the particulars relating to 
utilisation of material, labour and other items of cost in so- 
far as they are applicable to such other products or activi¬ 
ties, shall not be included in the cost of the items referred 
to in Rule 2, 

(2) The books of accounts referred to in sul>rulB (1) 
shall be kept on a tegular basis in such a way as I'o make 
it possible to calculate tlie cost of production and cost of 
sale of steel tubes or pipes of any quality, shape and/or 
sizei referred to in Rule 2 at regular intervals, say quart'erly, 
during the financial year (hereinafter referred to as the 
relevant period), as well as for the financial year as a whole, 
from the particulars entered therein and every such book 
of account and (he p'-oformae specified in Schedule IT shall 





3(i)] 


»ITOT¥TT?9nW:q^ 26, 198i/^lirs S, l906 


1183 


be completed not latei than ninety days from the end of 
the financial of the company to which they relate. 

(3) It shall be the duty of every person referred to in 
sub-section (6) and sub-section. (7) of section 209 of the 
said Act to take all reasonable steps to secure compliance 
by the company with the provisions of sub-rules (1) and 
(2) in ihc same manner as he is liable to rrsaintain accounts 
required under sub-section (1) of section 209 of the said 
Act. 

f4) Statistical and other records shall also bo maintained 
in compliance with the provisions of Schedule I and 11 which 
shall be such as to enable the company to exercise as far 
as possible, control over the various operations and costs 
with a view to achieve optimum economies in cosls and pro¬ 
vide the necessary data required by the cost auditor to suit¬ 
ably report on all the points referred to iq. the Cost Audit 
(Report) Rules, 1968, us amended from time to time. Such 
records shall be reconciled with the rcutrnj stibmitted to the 
Excise Departmpnt, DGTD and other authorities from lime 
to time. 

4. Penalty.—^If a company contravenes the provisions of 
Rule 3, tho company and every officer thcreo(, who is in 
default including the persons referred to in stih-nile (3) of 
Rule 3, shall, subject to the provisions of section 209 of 
the said Act, be puni.shable with fine, which moy extend 
to five hundred rupees and where the contravention is a 
continuing one, with a further fine which may extend to 
fifty rupees for every day after the first day during which 
such contravention continues.

(See Rule 3) 

I. Material 1.1 Direct Maleriab; Proper records shall be 
maintained showing separately all receipts, issues and balances 
both in. quantities and cost of steel required for the produc¬ 
tion. processing or manufacturing of products referred to 
in Rule 2. These reeord.s for direct material shall contain 
such details as to enable the company to determine the 
quantity, cost of receipts (including all direct charges upto 
the works), issues and balances of materials separately for 
imported and indigenous supplies. In case of imported mate¬ 
rials including those canalised through government agencies, 
details of FOB price, freight and insurance charges (CTF 
Value), customs duty, port charges, inland freight charges 
paid shall be recorded separ.ately. The basis on which the 
said quantities and costs of issues and consumption have 
been calculated shall be indicated in the cost records. 

J.2 Process Materials,—Proper records shall be maintained 
to show the receipts. Issues and balance, both ip quanitie* 
and costs of each item of proces.s materials, The costs of 
such process materials shall include nil direct charge® upto 
works. The issues and consumption shall be properly identi¬ 
fied with the Departments, Cost centres and products manu¬ 
factured, 

1.3 Recoveries of Process Chcmicab.—Proper records shall 
be maintained indicating the quantify and value of process 
materials recovered in the different processes. In the case of 
process materials thuj recovered which cannot he re-nsed 
in the process and are sold or dispo.sed of without further 
processing, the realisation from such sales shall be recorded 
and adjusted against the process concerned on a TensonaWo 
basis. In case further pioce.sing is necessarv to make these 
materials usable or saleable, as the case mav be, adequate 
records of cost involved for such further processing shall be 
maintained, 

1.4 Consumable Stores, Small tools, machinery spares 
.etc.-—1.4.1 Proper .records shall be maintainerl to ehov/ the 

receipts Issues and balances, both in quantities and cost of 
each item of consumable stores, small tools and machinerv 
snares.- dies. etc. The cost shown shall include all direct 
charges upto the works,

1.4.2 In the case of consumable stores, small tool* and 
machinery spares l^e costs of which are Insignificant, the 
company may. if it so desires, maintain such records for 
the main groups of such items. 

1.4.3 The cost of consumption of consumable stores, small 
tools, machinery spares, dies etc, shall be charged to the 
relevant cost centres on the basis of actual issues. Materials 
consumed on capital works such as additions to building*, 
plant and machinery and olhev fixed assets shall® be .shown 
under relevant capital heads. 

1.5 Wa.stages, Spoilages, Rejections, Losses, etc. of Mate¬ 
rials.— 

1.5.1 Proper records shall be maintained showing the 
quantity and value of wastages, spoilages, rejections and 
losses of materials in manufacture, consumable stores, small 
tools arirl machinery spares, dies etc. whether in transit, 
storage, mianufacture or at any other stage. The reasons 
tor wastages, spoilages, rejection and losses and method 
followed for the adjustment of the above losses shall be 
indicated in the cojt records. 

1.5.2. Proper records shall bo maintained showing the 
quantity of different kinds of scrap generated. . 

1.5.3. H any scrap is recycled, proper records in regard 
to quantity and reprocessing charges shall be maintained. 

1.5.4. Records for the realisation derived from the disposal 
of scrap rejected and waste material and the value of rejected 
materials reused in process, shall be maintained separately 
and the method adopted for adjusting the aforesaid recoveriea 
in the cost of production shall be indicated in the cost 
records. 

2. Wages and Salaries.— 

2.1 Proper records shall be maintained to show the atten¬ 
dance and earnings of all employees in each cost centre and 
tho work on which they are employed, The records shall 
also indicate separately for each cost centre;— 

(i) piece rate wages earned; 

(ii) incentive wages earned either individually or collec- . 
lively as production ^nn® or under any other 
scheme based on output; 

(iii) overtime wages earned; 

(,iv) earnings of castial labour. 

2.2 Idle time shall be separately recorded under classified' 
headings indicating the reasons therefor. The method followed 
for accounting of the idle time payments in determining the 
cost, of the product shall he disclosed in the cost records, 

2.3 Any wages and salaries allocable to capital works 
such as, additions to plant and noachinery, building, or other 
fixed a.ssets shall be accounted for under the relevant capital 
heads. 

3. Service department expenses; 

3.1 Datailed records shall be maintained to indicate ex- 
pslises incurred for each service department|cost' centre, 
like water supply, laboratory, welfare, transport, testing etc. 
These expenses shall be apportioned to other services and 
production departments on an equitable basis and applied 
consistently, 

3.2 Water.—Proper records showing the quantity and cost

4. Workshop/Repair & Maintenance.—4.1 Proper records 
showing the expenditure incurred by the workshop in repairs 
and maintenance jobs shall be maintained. The records .shall 
also indicate the basts of charging the.se expenses to different 

ost centres and departments. 

4.2 Expenditure on major repair works from which bene¬ 
fit is likely to accrue for more than oile financial year shall 
be .shown separately in the cost records indicating the method 
of allocating to the cojt of tubes and pipes manufactured 
during the relevant period. 

4.3 Expenditure incurred on works of a capital nature 
hall be capitalised. The cost of such jobs rhall include 
the expenditure on material, labour and due share of the 
overheads. 

4.4 The jobs carried dut by workshops of other units 
and for other units of the company shall be charged on a 
reasonable basis and applied consistently. 

5. Depreciation,—5,1 Proper records shall be maintained 
showing the cost and other particulars of fixed assets in res- 
Kct of which depreciation ig to be provided. These records 
shall inter alia, indicate th© cost of each item of asset includ¬ 
ing installation charges, if any, the date of installation and 
rate of deprecigtion and location of each asset. In respect 
of those a.ssets, the cost of which cannot be ascertained 
without any unreasonable expenditure or delay, the valuation 
shown in the hooks on the first day of the financial year 
beginning on or after the conunencement of these rules 

hall be taken as the cost. Such a valuation shall exclude 

valuation of any asset that had been done prior to the 
aforesaid date. 

5.2 The basis on which depreciation is calculated and 
allocated/apportioned to the vaiiong cost centres and depart¬ 
ments and absorbed on the products .shall be clearly indicated 
in the records. Depreciation chargeable to the different cost 
■ s and departments shall not be less than the amount 
af depreciation chargeahle in accordance with the provisions 
if suh-section 12) of Section 205 of the said Act, and *hall 
relate to plant, mochinerv and other fixed assets utilised in 
such cost centres and departments. In the case of assets 
nd group of assets on which depreciation is -written off at 
he rate of 100 per cent in the relevant year otherwise than 
as provided for in the Income Tax Act 1961 (43 of 1961) 
and the ndes made thereunder such depreciation shall be 
oread over the number of years doting which benefit Is 
derived from such, assets or group of such assets. In case 
he amount of depreciation charged in the cost records in 


any financial year is higher than the amount of depreci^on 
chai'geable under the aforesaid provisions of the Compancy 
Act, the amount so charged in excess shall be indicated 
clearly in the cost records. The cumulative depreciation 
charged in the cost records against any individual item of 
asset shall not, however, exceed the original cost of the 
respective asset. 

6. Royalty/Technical know-how fee.—^Adequate records 
shall be maintained showing the royalty or other recuning 
or non-recurring payments made to collaborators or techno¬ 
logy suppliers in terms of agreements entered into with 
them. Such records shall be kept separately in respect of 
each party. The basis of charging such amounts including 
onetime payments, to the products shall be indicated in the 
cost records. 

7. Other Overheads.—7.1 Proper records shall be maintain. 
ed showing the various items of expenses comprising the 
overheads. These expenses shall be analysed, classified and 
grouped into works, administration and selling and distribu¬ 
tion overheads. In case any expenses included in the above 
categories of ovcrhead.s can he identified with a particular 
activity or products, such expenses shall be Eegrcgated and 
charged to relevant activity or product at the first instahee 
and thereafter the remaining common expenses under the 
above categories of overheads shall be apportioned on reason¬ 
able and equitable basis cod applied consistently. The method 
followed for aDocation/absorption of the above categories 
of oveiheads to the departments/cost centres/prodnets shall 
be indicated in the co.st records, 

7.2 Where the company is engaged in the manufacture 
of any other products in addition to the products referred to 
in Rule; 2. the records shall clearly indicate the basis follow¬ 
ed for apportionment of the common overheads including 
head office expenses of the company to the steel tubes and 
pipes activities, other activities and capital works. The hash 
followed for apportionment/nb-sorption of overheads to the 
cost centres and products shall be indicated in the cost 
records. 

8. Conversion Cost,—Proper records shall be maintained 
for splitting up of conversion costs (the cost of manufacture 
less direct material cost) into fixed and variable costs for 
filling the relevant proforrme under schedule II. 

9. Interest.—^Proper records shall be maintained showing 
interest charges. 'The amount of interest shall be allocated 
to steel tubes and pipes and other activities on a reasonable 
and equitable basis which is tp be followed consistently. 
The basis of such allocation shall be spelt out clearly in 
the steel tubes and pipes cost. Basis for further apjmrtion- 
ment of the share of iuteiest for steel tubes and pipes of 
different sizes, grades cud qualities thereof shall also be 
reasonable and equitable and the same shall be followed 
consistently. 

10. Expenses/Incentives on Export,—Proper records show¬ 
ing the expenses incurred on the; export of steel tubes and 
pipes, if any. shall be separately maintained, so that the 
cost of export sales can be determined correctly. The ex¬ 
penses incurred on exports as -well as any export incentive 
earned shall be reflected in the cost statement relating to 
export sales. Export incentive shall bo treated as other 
income and reflected in the cost records as such, 

11. Steel Tubes & Pipes transferred for captive consump- 
x'ion.—Proper records shall be maintained showing the quan¬ 
tity and cost of steel tubes and pipes transferred to other 
departments/units of the company for self-consumption. Such 
transfer shall ordinarily be effected at cost to he disclosed 
in the cost records. If. however, the transfer of steel tubes 
and pipes is made for erptive consumption at a valuation 
other than cost, the notional profit or loss arising out of 
such transfer ^all also he disclosed in the cost records.

followed for determining the cost of work-in-progress and,

so as to reveal the cost elements that have been taken into 
account in such computation. The ir«:thod adopted shall be

MINISTRY OF HOME AFFAIRS 


4. Disqualification:—No person— 


(Department of Personnel and Administrative Refortru) 
New Delhi, the 28th April, 1984 


(a) Who, has entered into or contracted a marriage 
with a person having a spouse living, or 


G,S.R. 507.—In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of 
recruitment to the post of Private Secretary to Director at 
the Lai Bahadur Shastri National Academy of Administra¬ 
tion, Mussoorie in the Department of Persomiel & Adminis¬ 
trative Reforms, Ministry of Homs Affairs, namely.— 

1. Short title and commencement;—(i) These rules may 
be called the Lai Bahadur Shastri National Academy of 
Administration, Mussoorie (Office Establishment Posts) 
Ainsndment Rules, 1984. 

(11) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication In the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, classification and seals of pay:—The 
number of the said post, its classification and the scale of 
pay attached thereto shall be as specified In Columns 2 to 
4 to the Schedule hereto annexed. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifica¬ 
tions;—^The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters relating to the said post shall be as spe¬ 
cified in cohuuns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule, 


(b) Who, having a sjwuse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government' may, if satisfied 
that such maniage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and tlje other party to ihc n^r- 
riage and that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt 
any peirson ^m the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax:—^Wbere the Central Government is 
of the opinion ffiat it is necessary or exf^dient so to do, 
it may, by order, for reasons to b«; recorded in writing 
and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commis¬ 
sion, relax any of the provisions of these rules with ree- 
pect to any class or category of persons. 

6, Saving,—Nothing in these rules shall affect reserva¬ 
tions, relaxation of age limit, and other concessions arc 
required to.be provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Sche¬ 
duled Tribes and other special categories of persons in ac¬ 
cordance with the orders issued by the Central Government 
from time to time in this regard.

MINISTRY OF PLANNING 
(DEPARTMENT OF STATISTICS) 

New Delhi, the 2rid May, 1984 

G.S.R. 508.— In exercise of the powers conlencJ by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Superintendent (Statistics), in the Data Processing Division and 
Survey Design and Research Division, National Sample Survey Organisation in the Department of Statistics under the Ministry of Pla n 
ning, namely :- 

1 . Short title and commencement.—(1) These rules may be called the Nati ual Sample Survey Organisation Data Processing 
Division and Survey Design and Research Division (Superintendent) (Statistics) Recruitment Rules, 1984. 

(2) These rules shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of posts, classification'and scale of pay,- -The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3 _ Method of recruitment age limit and qualifications, etc:—The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other 

matters relating to the said post sltall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule. 

4. Disqualification -No person 

fa) Who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse iving, or 

fb) Who, having a spouse iving, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for appoint- 
mont to the said post: Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such a marriage is permissible under the personal 
law applicable to such person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from 
the operaiion of this rule. 





[wr 3{i)] 


1 199 


Tfirr^: 25, 1984/75':5 5, 1909 


5. Power to relax—Where the Central Govornmtut is of the opnion that it is necessary' or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation wi<h the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the provisions 
of these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving—-Nothins in those rules shall affect reservations, relaxation of age limit and other concessions required to bo 
provided for the Scheduled Castes, the Schedules Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance jvith the orders issued 
by the Central Government from time to time in this regard.

and Class II posts) Recruitment Rules, 1973 Orerelnafter referred to as the said rules), for the words and figures “Class I” and Class If, 
wherever occurlng, the words and letters "Group A” and “Group B” shall be substlmted respectively.

DEPARTMENT OF SUPPLY 
New Delhi, the 5th April, 1984 

G.S.R. 510-—In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Security Guard in the Office of Director of Supplies and Disposais 
Bombay, namely 

1. Short title and commencement <1) These rules may be called the Director of Supplies and Disposals, Bombay (Security 
Guard) Recruitment Rules, 1984. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Officiai Gazette. 

2. Number of posts, classification and scales of pay ;—The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in the column 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed to those rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit qualifications, etc.—^Thc method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other matters 
relating to the said post shall bo as specified in column 5 to 14 of the said Schedule. 

4. Disqualification No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) Mro, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided tliat the Central Oovornment may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to 
such person and the other party to the mrriago and that there arc other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the 
of operation of this rule. 






1206 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : MAY 2 6,1 984/JYAISTHA 5.1 906 [Part II~Sec. 3®] 

5. Power to relax Where the Central Government is of the opinion that it Is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by order 
and for reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any of the provisions of these rules with resDcct to any class or categor of persons, 

6. Saving Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, relaxations of ago limit and other concessions required to be pro¬ 
vided for candidates belonging (o the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special ctegories in accordance with the orders 
issued by the Central Government from time to time in this regard.

and suggestions from? all persoixs likely to be irfTcctcd there¬ 
by till the 9th February, 1984;

public on the 25th February, 1984. 

And whereas no objections or suggestions have been re¬ 
ceived;

section 28 of the Ihdian Boilers Act, 1923 (5 of 1923),

1950, namely:— 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Indian Boi¬ 

lers (Amendment) Ro^gulations, 1984, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette.

DEPARTMENT OF ENVIRONMENT 
(National Museuni of Natural History) 
New Delhi, the 8th May, 1984 


G.S.R. 513.—In exercise of the powers, conferred by 
the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution and in super¬ 
session of Department of Science and Technology, Natural 
History Museum (Group ‘A’ and Group ‘B’) Recruitment 
Rules, 1975, in so far as they relate to the post of Planning 
Officer, except as respects things done or omitted to bo 
done before such supersession, the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to 
certain Group ‘A’ and Group ‘B’ posts in the National 
Museum of Natural History, Department of Environment, 
namely ;— 


1. Short title and commencement.— (i) These rules may 
be called the Department of Environment, National Museum 
Of ^Natural History (Group ‘A’ and Group ‘B’ posts) Re¬ 
cruitment Rules, 1984. 

(ij) They shall come into force on the date of their publi¬ 
cation in the Officiai Gazette, 


2. Application .—These rules shall apply to the posts speci¬ 
fied in column I of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 


3. Number of posts, classification and scales of pay.— 
The nuinber of the saM psts, their classification and th( 
scales of pay attached, thereto, shall be as specified it 
column.s 2 to 4 of the said Schedule. 


4, Method of recruitment, age limit, other quedifications, 
etc.—The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications 
and other matters relating to the said psts shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Scheduled aforesaid. 

5. Disqualificatxons.-^No person.— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a jnaniage 
with a person having a spouse living, or 


(b) who having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with any person sh^ be 
eligible for appointment to any of the said pasts; 


Kovided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli¬ 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and that there are other grounds for so doing exempt any 
person from the operation of this rule. 


6. Power to relax—^Wbere the Central Government is 
of the opinion 4hat it is necessary or expedient so to do, it 
may, by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and 
In consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, 
relax any of the provisions of these rules with respect to 
any class or category of persons. 

r , 

7. Saving.—Nothing in these rules shall affect reservationr, 
reJaxation of age limit and other concessiona rcQuired to be 
provided for the Scheduled Castes^ and Scheduled Tribes 
and other special categories of persons in accordance with 
the orders issued by the Central Government from time 
to time in this regard,.

MINISTRY OF SJ-UPPING AND TRANSPORT 
(Hstt. Section) 

New Delhi, the 26th Apiil, 1984 

G.S.R, 514.—In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution and in supersession 
of the Ministry of Shipping and Transport (Transport Wing) 
Shipping Coordination and Chartering Organisation (Class 
1 & II Recruitment Rules)? 1974 except as jespects things 
done or omitted to be done before such supersession, the 
President hereby makes the following rules regulating the 
method of recruitment of Group A and B posts m the Char¬ 
tering Wing of the Ministry of Shipping and Transport 
namely :—■ 

1. vShort title and commencement:—^(i) These rules may 
be called the Miitistry of Shipping and Transport (F^stablish- 
ment Section), Chartering Wing (Group A A B) Recruitment 
Rules, 1984, 

(ii) They shall coitje into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the QfUcial Gazette. 

2, Application:—These rules shall apply to the po.sts 
specified in column 1 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Number of posts classification and sc.nl of pay.—The 
number of the said posts, their classification and the scales of 
pay attached thereto, shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of 
the said Schedule. 


1237 


4. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc.:— 
The method of recruitment, age limit, qualification and other 
matters relating to the said posts shall be as specified in 
column 5 to 13 of the said Schedule. 

5. Disqualificatiorts No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to any of the said posts; 

Provided that the Central Government, if satisfied that 
.such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli¬ 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and there arc other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
Irom the operation of this rule. 

6. Power to relax.—Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that ft is necessary or expedient so to do it may by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation 
with thc Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the 
provisions of these rules with respect to any class or catcgoiy 
of persons or the posts. 

7. Saving.—^Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
relaxations of age limit and other concession required to be 
provided for the Schedule Castes, Schedule Tribes and other 
special categories of the persons in accordance with the orders 
issued by the Central Government from time to time in this 
regard.

MINISTRY OF INFORMATION & BROADCASTING 
New Delhi, the 5th May, 1984 

O.S.R. 515 -—In exercise of the powers conferred by the prevision to Article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Chief Executive OflBoer in the Board of Film Certification 
under the Ministry of Information and Broadc isting, namely 

1. Short title and commencement (1) These rules may be called the Board of Film Certification (Chief Executive Officer) Re¬ 
cruitment Rules, 1984. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date oftheir publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of posts, classification and scale of Pay The number of post, its classification and the scale of pay attached there' 
to shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Method of recruitment, age-limit, qualification etc. The method of recruitment to the said post, age-limit, qualifications 
and other matters connected therewith shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4, Disqualification :^No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b who, having a spouse living, has entered i nto or contracted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post ; 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party lO the marriage and there ate other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this ruler. 

5, Power to relax :—Where the Central Government is of oplnionthat it is necessary or expedient so 10 do, it may by order for 
reasons to be recorded in writing, and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the provisions of these rules 
with respect to any class or category of persons. 

Saving Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations, relaxation of age-limit and other concessions required to te provider 
for candidates belonging to tbe Scheduled Castes , the Slcheduled Tribes and Other special categories of persons in accordance with the 
Orders issued by the Central Government from time to time in this' regard.

firmation).

shall be held.

New Delhi, the 5th May, 1984 

G,S.R, 516,—Ta exorcise of the powers conferred by the 
■ proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the followina rules further to amend the Indian 
Posts and Telegraphs (Clerks in Savina Bank Control and 
Internal Check Organisation) Recruitment Rules, 1968, 
namely:—• 

(1) These rules may be called the Indian Posts and Tele¬ 

graphs (Clerks in Saving Bank Control and internal 
Check Oiganisation) Recruitment (Amendment) 
Rules, 1984. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Indian Posts and Telegraphs 
Clerks in Savings Bank Control and Internal Check Organisation) 
Recruitment Rules, 196.8; 

(a) against serial number I relating to the post of Lower 
Division, Clerks in the Savings Bank Control and 
Internal Check Organisation, in Circles, in column 
5,— 

(i) the words “oil the 1st July of the year of recruit¬ 
ment” sfbll be omittted; 

fii) the following note shall be added, namely:— 

“Note;—The crucial date for determining the age 
limit shall, in each case be the closing date for 
receipt of applications from candidates in India 
(other than those in the Union Territories of 
the Andaman and Nicobar Islands a*d Lak¬ 
shadweep). 

In the case of recruitment made through the Employment 
Exchange the crucial data for determining the age limif shall 
be the last dale upto which the Employment Exchange Is 
asked to submit the names. 

(b) against serial number 2 relating to the post of Upper 
Division Clcrla in the Saving Bank Control and In¬ 
ternal Check Organisation in Circles, in column 5,— 

(i) the words “as on 1st July of the year of the re¬ 
cruitment” shall he omitted; 

(ii) the following note shall be added, namely;— 
“Note;—^Thc crucial date for datermining the age 

limit shall, in each case be the closing date for 
receipt of applications from candidates in India 
(other than those in the Union TciTitories of 
the Andaman and Nicobar Island; and Laksha¬ 
dweep). 

In the case of recruitment made through the Employment 
Exchange, the crucial date for determining the age limit shall 
be the last date upto which the Employment Exchange is asked 
to submit the names. 


Note ' The Indian Post's and Telegraphs (Clerks in Savings 
Bank Control and Internal Check Organisation) Recruitment 
Rules, 1968 were published with notification bearing GSR 
817 dated 22-1-1969. Subsequently these rules were amend¬ 
ed by GSR 160 dated 10-My72,/GSR 934 dated 11-7-1972. 
GSR 937 dated 14-7-1972 and GSR 725 dated 3-8-1982,

G.S.Jl. 517.—^In exercise of the powers conferred by tbe 
provisoNo article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the followirta rules further to amend the Indian Posts 
and Telegraphs (Postmen/Mailgnard/Head Mailguards) re¬ 
cruitment Rules, 1969 iiarw“lv :-- 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Indian Posts and 
Telegi-aphs (Postmcn/Mailguards/Head Mailguards) 
Recruitment ("Amendment) Rules, 1984. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazelle, 


2. In the Schedule to the Indian Posts and Telegraphs 
(Postmen/Mailguards/Head Mailgugrds) Recuritment Rule.s, 
1969 against serial number 1 and 2 relating to the posts of 
against serial number 1 and 2 relating to the posts of 
“Postman and village Postman” and “Mailgpards” res¬ 
pectively in column, 5, the word “as on Ist Inly of the year 
of recruitment,”’ shall be omitted and the following note shall 
be added, namely:—

“Islote:—The crucial date for datermining the age

and Lakshadweep), In the case of recruitment 
made through the Employment Exchange, the
